### Ms. V.

<ruby><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>YIÆ-ʽO-WÔ</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：

<sup>2</sup> 「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孫</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt><rb>大麻風</rb><rt>da-mô-fong</rt><rb>病個</rb><rt>bing-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>白濁</rb><rt>bah-djoh</rt><rb>病個</rb><rt>bing-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>摸着</rb><rt>moh-djoh</rt><rb>屍首</rb><rt>s-siu</rt><rb>遭</rb><rt>tsao</rt><rb>污穢個</rb><rt>u-w̆e-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>趕出</rb><rt>ken-cʽih</rt><rb>營寨</rb><rt>ying-dza</rt><rb>外</rb><rt>nga</rt></ruby>。

<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>無論</rb><rt>Vu-leng</rt><rb>男</rb><rt>nen</rt><rb>女</rb><rt>nyü</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>趕出</rb><rt>ken-cʽih</rt><rb>營寨</rb><rt>ying-dza</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>未免</rb><rt>vi-min</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>污穢</rb><rt>u-w̆e</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>庉的個</rb><rt>deng-tih-go</rt><rb>營寨</rb><rt>ying-dza</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>於是</rb><rt>Ü-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孫</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>將</rb><rt>tsiang</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>趕出</rb><rt>ken-cʽih</rt><rb>營寨</rb><rt>ying-dza</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u>，<u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孫</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>。

<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：

<sup>6</sup> 「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孫</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗論</rb><rt>feh-leng</rt><rb>男</rb><rt>nen</rt><rb>女</rb><rt>nyü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>犯着</rb><rt>væn-djoh</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>素常</rb><rt>su-djông</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>犯個</rb><rt>væn-go</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>觸犯</rb><rt>cʽoh-væn</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>陷落</rb><rt>ʽæn-lôh</rt><rb>罪裏</rb><rt>ze-li</rt></ruby>。

<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>Ziu</rt><rb>當該</rb><rt>tông-kæ</rt><rb>招認</rb><rt>tsiao-jing</rt><rb>自個</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>將</rb><rt>tsiang</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>虧待</rb><rt>kwʽe-dæ</rt><rb>人個</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>還</rb><rt>wæn</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>並且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>另外</rb><rt>ling-nga</rt><rb>加湊</rb><rt>kô-tsʽeo</rt><rb>五股之一</rb><rt>ng-kwu-ts-ih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>歸撥</rb><rt>kwe-peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>虧待個</rb><rt>kwʽe-dæ-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。

<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>嘸有</rb><rt>m-yiu</rt><rb>戚族</rb><rt>tsʽih-dzoh</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>接受</rb><rt>tsih-ziu</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>還個</rb><rt>wæn-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>該</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>將</rb><rt>tsiang</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>還個</rb><rt>wæn-go</rt><rb>獻</rb><rt>hyin</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>歸撥</rb><rt>kwe-peh</rt><rb>祭司</rb><rt>tsi-s</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>另外</rb><rt>ling-nga</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>獻</rb><rt>hyin</rt><rb>一隻</rb><rt>ih-tsah</rt><rb>雄羊</rb><rt>yüong-yiang</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>贖罪</rb><rt>joh-ze</rt><rb>祭</rb><rt>tsi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>替</rb><rt>tʽi</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>贖罪</rb><rt>joh-ze</rt></ruby>。

<sup>9</sup> <u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孫</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>尊起</rb><rt>tseng-kyʽi</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>誠心</rb><rt>dzing-sing</rt><rb>舉上個</rb><rt>kyü-zông-go</rt><rb>祭</rb><rt>tsi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>馱進</rb><rt>do-tsing</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>祭司個</rb><rt>tsi-s-go</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>禮物</rb><rt>li-veh</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>歸撥</rb><rt>kwe-peh</rt><rb>祭司</rb><rt>tsi-s</rt></ruby>。

<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>Nying</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>尊起</rb><rt>tseng-kyʽi</rt><rb>誠心個</rb><rt>dzing-sing-go</rt><rb>禮物</rb><rt>li-veh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>歸撥</rb><rt>kwe-peh</rt><rb>祭司</rb><rt>tsi-s</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>送</rb><rt>song</rt><rb>禮物</rb><rt>li-veh</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>何裏</rb><rt>ʽah-li</rt><rb>一位</rb><rt>ih-we</rt><rb>祭司</rb><rt>tsi-s</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>歸撥</rb><rt>kwe-peh</rt><rb>何裏</rb><rt>ʽah-li</rt><rb>一位</rb><rt>ih-we</rt><rb>祭司</rb><rt>tsi-s</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：

<sup>12</sup> 「<ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>Yüong</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孫</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>人個</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>妻子</rb><rt>tsʽi-ts</rt><rb>行爲</rb><rt>ʽang-we</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>端正</rb><rt>tön-tsing</rt><rb>觸犯</rb><rt>cʽoh-væn</rt><rb>丈夫</rb><rt>dziang-fu</rt></ruby>：

<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>犯</rb><rt>væn</rt><rb>姦淫</rb><rt>kæn-ying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>污穢</rb><rt>u-w̆e</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>丈夫</rb><rt>dziang-fu</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>得知</rb><rt>teh-cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>嘸有</rb><rt>m-yiu</rt><rb>別人</rb><rt>bih-nying</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>對證</rb><rt>te-tsing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>嘸有</rb><rt>m-yiu</rt><rb>當時</rb><rt>tông-z</rt><rb>抲着</rb><rt>kʽô-djoh</rt></ruby>。

<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>丈夫</rb><rt>Dziang-fu</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>嫉妒</rb><rt>gyih-tu</rt><rb>之心</rb><rt>ts-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>嫉妒</rb><rt>gyih-tu</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>妻子</rb><rt>tsʽi-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>妻子</rb><rt>tsʽi-ts</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>污穢</rb><rt>u-w̆e</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽÔh-tsia</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>嫉妒</rb><rt>gyih-tu</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>妻子</rb><rt>tsʽi-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>不過</rb><rt>peh-ko</rt><rb>妻子</rb><rt>tsʽi-ts</rt><rb>並</rb><rt>bing</rt><rb>嘸有</rb><rt>m-yiu</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>污穢</rb><rt>u-w̆e</rt></ruby>；

<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>該</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>帶</rb><rt>ta</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>妻子</rb><rt>tsʽi-ts</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>祭司個</rb><rt>tsi-s-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>該</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>馱</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>禮物</rb><rt>li-veh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>以法</rb><rt>yi-fah</rt><rb>十股</rb><rt>jih-kwu</rt><rb>之一</rb><rt>ts-ih</rt><rb>大麥粉</rb><rt>do-mah-feng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>澆</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>油</rb><rt>yiu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>加</rb><rt>kô</rt><rb>香料</rb><rt>hyiang-liao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>箇是</rb><rt>keh-z</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>嫉妒</rb><rt>gyih-tu</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>記得</rb><rt>kyi-teh</rt><rb>罪個</rb><rt>ze-go</rt><rb>禮物</rb><rt>li-veh</rt></ruby>。

<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>祭司</rb><rt>Tsi-s</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt><rb>走攏來</rb><rt>tseo-long-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>立</rb><rt>lih</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>耶和華個</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô-go</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt></ruby>。

<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>祭司</rb><rt>Tsi-s</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>該</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>將</rb><rt>tsiang</rt><rb>誠心個</rb><rt>dzing-sing-go</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>𣥼來</rb><rt>tsi-læ</rt><rb>瓦器皿</rb><rt>ngô-kyʽi-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>帳篷</rb><rt>tsiang-bong</rt><rb>地上</rb><rt>di-zông</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>馱</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>灰塵</rb><rt>hwe-dzing</rt><rb>安</rb><rt>en</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>水裏</rb><rt>shü-li</rt></ruby>；

<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>Wa-yiu</rt><rb>祭司</rb><rt>tsi-s</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>將</rb><rt>tsiang</rt><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt><rb>立</rb><rt>lih</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>拆散</rb><rt>tsʽah-sæn</rt><rb>女人個</rb><rt>nyü-nying-go</rt><rb>頭髮</rb><rt>deo-fah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>嫉妒</rb><rt>gyih-tu</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>記得</rb><rt>kyi-teh</rt><rb>罪個</rb><rt>ze-go</rt><rb>祭物</rb><rt>tsi-veh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>放</rb><rt>fông</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>女人個</rb><rt>nyü-nying-go</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>祭司</rb><rt>tsi-s</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>手裏</rb><rt>siu-li</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>馱</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>咒詛個</rb><rt>tsiu-cü-go</rt><rb>苦水</rb><rt>kwʽu-shü</rt></ruby>；

<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>祭司</rb><rt>Tsi-s</rt><rb>還要</rb><rt>wa-iao</rt><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt><rb>罰咒</rb><rt>vah-tsiu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>嘸有</rb><rt>m-yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>犯</rb><rt>væn</rt><rb>姦淫</rb><rt>kæn-ying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>嘸有</rb><rt>m-yiu</rt><rb>違背</rb><rt>we-be</rt><rb>丈夫</rb><rt>dziang-fu</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>端正</rb><rt>tön-tsing</rt><rb>污穢</rb><rt>u-w̆e</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>可以</rb><rt>kʽo-yi</rt><rb>免掉</rb><rt>min-diao</rt><rb>咒詛</rb><rt>tsiu-cü</rt><rb>苦水個</rb><rt>kwʽu-shü-go</rt><rb>災難</rb><rt>tsæ-næn</rt></ruby>；

<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>不過</rb><rt>Peh-ko</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>果然</rb><rt>ko-jün</rt><rb>違背</rb><rt>we-be</rt><rb>丈夫</rb><rt>dziang-fu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>端正</rb><rt>tön-tsing</rt><rb>污穢</rb><rt>u-w̆e</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>丈夫</rb><rt>dziang-fu</rt><rb>之外</rb><rt>ts-nga</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>別人</rb><rt>bih-nying</rt><rb>近身</rb><rt>gying-sing</rt></ruby>；

<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>Keh-tsao</rt><rb>祭司</rb><rt>tsi-s</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt><rb>罰咒</rb><rt>vah-tsiu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>祭司</rb><rt>tsi-s</rt><rb>還要</rb><rt>wa-iao</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>罰咒</rb><rt>vah-tsiu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就有</rb><rt>ziu-yiu</rt><rb>咒詛</rb><rt>tsiu-cü</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>百姓個</rb><rt>pah-sing-go</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>腳腿</rb><rt>kyiah-tʽe</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>爛</rb><rt>læn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>肚皮</rb><rt>du-bi</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>脹</rb><rt>tsiang</rt></ruby>；

<sup>22</sup> <ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>Wa-yiu</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>咒詛個</rb><rt>tsiu-cü-go</rt><rb>苦水</rb><rt>kwʽu-shü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>入</rb><rt>jih</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>肚腸</rb><rt>du-dziang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>肚皮</rb><rt>du-bi</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>脹</rb><rt>tsiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>腳腿</rb><rt>kyiah-tʽe</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>爛</rb><rt>læn</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt><rb>還要</rb><rt>wa-iao</rt><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>亞門</rb><rt>Üô-meng</rt></ruby></u>，<u><ruby><rb>亞門</rb><rt>Üô-meng</rt></ruby></u>。

<sup>23</sup> <ruby><rb>祭司</rb><rt>Tsi-s</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>將</rb><rt>tsiang</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>咒詛</rb><rt>tsiu-cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>寫來</rb><rt>sia-læ</rt><rb>書裏</rb><rt>shü-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>還要</rb><rt>wa-iao</rt><rb>馱</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>苦水</rb><rt>kwʽu-shü</rt><rb>塗抹</rb><rt>du-mah</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>24</sup> <ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>Yi</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt><rb>喝</rb><rt>hah</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>咒詛個</rb><rt>tsiu-cü-go</rt><rb>苦水</rb><rt>kwʽu-shü</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>一</rb><rt>ih</rt><rb>入進</rb><rt>jih-tsing</rt><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>成爲</rb><rt>dzing-we</rt><rb>苦水</rb><rt>kwʽu-shü</rt></ruby>。

<sup>25</sup> <ruby><rb>祭司</rb><rt>Tsi-s</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>馱</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>嫉妒</rb><rt>gyih-tu</rt><rb>心個</rb><rt>sing-go</rt><rb>素祭</rb><rt>su-tsi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>搖一搖</rb><rt>yiao-ih-yiao</rt><rb>帶到</rb><rt>ta-tao</rt><rb>祭壇</rb><rt>tsi-dæn</rt><rb>沿</rb><rt>yin</rt></ruby>。

<sup>27</sup> <ruby><rb>水</rb><rt>Shü</rt><rb>喝好仔</rb><rt>hah-hao-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>果然</rb><rt>ko-jün</rt><rb>受</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>污穢</rb><rt>u-w̆e</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>得罪</rb><rt>teh-ze</rt><rb>丈夫</rb><rt>dziang-fu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>咒詛個</rb><rt>tsiu-cü-go</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>裏頭</rb><rt>li-deo</rt><rb>成爲</rb><rt>dzing-we</rt><rb>苦水</rb><rt>kwʽu-shü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>肚皮</rb><rt>du-bi</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>脹</rb><rt>tsiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>腳腿</rb><rt>kyiah-tʽe</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>爛</rb><rt>læn</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>keh-tsao</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>咒詛個</rb><rt>tsiu-cü-go</rt></ruby>。

<sup>28</sup> <ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>Ziah</rt><rb>嘸有</rb><rt>m-yiu</rt><rb>受</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>污穢</rb><rt>u-w̆e</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>實在</rb><rt>jih-dzæ</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>清潔個</rb><rt>tsʽing-kyih-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>keh-tsao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>自由自在</rb><rt>zi-yiu-zi-dzæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>並且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>身孕</rb><rt>sing-yüing</rt></ruby>。

<sup>29</sup> <ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>嫉妒</rb><rt>gyih-tu</rt><rb>之心個</rb><rt>ts-sing-go</rt><rb>規條</rb><rt>kwe-diao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>弗端正</rb><rt>feh-tön-tsing</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>受</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>污穢個</rb><rt>u-w̆e-go</rt></ruby>；

<sup>30</sup> <ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽÔh-tsia</rt><rb>丈夫</rb><rt>dziang-fu</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>嫉妒</rb><rt>gyih-tu</rt><rb>之心</rb><rt>ts-sing</rt><rb>所以</rb><rt>sô-yi</rt><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>嫉妒</rb><rt>gyih-tu</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>妻子</rb><rt>tsʽi-ts</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>keh-tsao</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt><rb>立</rb><rt>lih</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>祭司</rb><rt>tsi-s</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>辦</rb><rt>bæn</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>放</rb><rt>fông</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>31</sup> <ruby><rb>丈夫</rb><rt>Dziang-fu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>嘸罪個</rb><rt>m-ze-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt><rb>必要</rb><rt>pih-iao</rt><rb>承當</rb><rt>dzing-tông</rt><rb>自個</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt></ruby>。」

### Ms. VI.

<ruby><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>YIÆ-ʽO-WÔ</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：

<sup>2</sup> 「<ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>Yüong</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孫</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>無論</rb><rt>vu-leng</rt><rb>男</rb><rt>nen</rt><rb>女</rb><rt>nyü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>許願</rb><rt>hyü-nyün</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>誠心</rb><rt>dzing-sing</rt><rb>歸撥</rb><rt>kwe-peh</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt></ruby>。

<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>老酒</rb><rt>Lao-tsiu</rt><rb>燒酒</rb><rt>siao-tsiu</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>弗可</rb><rt>feh-kʽo</rt><rb>喝</rb><rt>hah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>醋個</rb><rt>tsʽu-go</rt><rb>酒</rb><rt>tsiu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>連</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>葡萄</rb><rt>bu-dao</rt><rb>汁水</rb><rt>tsih-shü</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>弗可</rb><rt>feh-kʽo</rt><rb>喝</rb><rt>hah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>鮮葡萄</rb><rt>sin-bu-dao</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>葡萄乾</rb><rt>bu-dao-ken</rt><rb>弗可</rb><rt>feh-kʽo</rt><rb>喫</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt></ruby>。

<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>在</rb><rt>Dzæ</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>誠心個</rb><rt>dzing-sing-go</rt><rb>日子</rb><rt>nyih-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>葡萄</rb><rt>bu-dao</rt><rb>樹上</rb><rt>jü-zông</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>結個</rb><rt>kyih-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗可</rb><rt>feh-kʽo</rt><rb>喫</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>葡萄個</rb><rt>bu-dao-go</rt><rb>𣝗</rb><rt>weh</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>葡萄個</rb><rt>bu-dao-go</rt><rb>皮</rb><rt>bi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>弗可</rb><rt>feh-kʽo</rt><rb>喫</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt></ruby>。

<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>在</rb><rt>Dzæ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>許願個</rb><rt>hyü-nyün-go</rt><rb>日子</rb><rt>nyih-ts</rt><rb>弗可</rb><rt>feh-kʽo</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>刀</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>剃頭</rb><rt>tʽi-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>頭髮</rb><rt>deo-fah</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>隨其</rb><rt>ze-gyi</rt><rb>興起</rb><rt>hying-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等到</rb><rt>teng-tao</rt><rb>誠心</rb><rt>dzing-sing</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>日子</rb><rt>nyih-ts</rt><rb>滿足</rb><rt>mun-tsoh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>成爲</rb><rt>dzing-we</rt><rb>聖善</rb><rt>sing-jün</rt></ruby>。

<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>在</rb><rt>Dzæ</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>尊起</rb><rt>tseng-kyʽi</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>耶和華個</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô-go</rt><rb>日子</rb><rt>nyih-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗可</rb><rt>feh-kʽo</rt><rb>近攏</rb><rt>gying-long</rt><rb>屍首</rb><rt>s-siu</rt></ruby>。

<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>Ziu-z</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>父母</rb><rt>vu-meo</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt><rb>姊妹</rb><rt>tsi-me</rt><rb>死了</rb><rt>si-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弗可</rb><rt>feh-kʽo</rt><rb>近攏</rb><rt>gying-long</rt><rb>遭</rb><rt>tsao</rt><rb>污穢</rb><rt>u-w̆e</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>養</rb><rt>yiang</rt><rb>頭髮</rb><rt>deo-fah</rt><rb>尊起</rb><rt>tseng-kyʽi</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt></ruby>。

<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>在</rb><rt>Dzæ</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>尊起個</rb><rt>tseng-kyʽi-go</rt><rb>日子</rb><rt>nyih-ts</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt><rb>誠心個</rb><rt>dzing-sing-go</rt></ruby>。

<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>Ziah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>忽然</rb><rt>hweh-jün</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>旁邊</rb><rt>bông-pin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>污穢</rb><rt>u-w̆e</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>誠心個</rb><rt>dzing-sing-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>當該</rb><rt>tông-kæ</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>第七日</rb><rt>di-tsʽih-nyih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>成爲</rb><rt>dzing-we</rt><rb>清潔個</rb><rt>tsʽing-kyih-go</rt><rb>日子</rb><rt>nyih-ts</rt><rb>剃頭</rb><rt>tʽi-deo</rt></ruby>。

<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>第八</rb><rt>Di-pah</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt><rb>應該</rb><rt>ing-kæ</rt><rb>帶</rb><rt>ta</rt><rb>兩隻</rb><rt>liang-tsah</rt><rb>斑鳩</rb><rt>pæn-kyiu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>兩隻</rb><rt>liang-tsah</rt><rb>小</rb><rt>siao</rt><rb>鵓鴿</rb><rt>beh-keh</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>會幕</rb><rt>we-mo</rt><rb>門口</rb><rt>meng-kʽeo</rt><rb>交代</rb><rt>kao-dæ</rt><rb>祭司</rb><rt>tsi-s</rt></ruby>。

<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>祭司</rb><rt>Tsi-s</rt><rb>該</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>將</rb><rt>tsiang</rt><rb>一隻</rb><rt>ih-tsah</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>贖罪祭</rb><rt>joh-ze-tsi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一隻</rb><rt>ih-tsah</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>燒化祭</rb><rt>siao-hwô-tsi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>替其</rb><rt>tʽi-gyi</rt><rb>贖罪</rb><rt>joh-ze</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>近攏</rb><rt>gying-long</rt><rb>屍首</rb><rt>s-siu</rt><rb>遭</rb><rt>tsao</rt><rb>污穢個</rb><rt>u-w̆e-go</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>頭</rb><rt>deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇日</rb><rt>keh-nyih</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>成爲</rb><rt>dzing-we</rt><rb>聖善</rb><rt>sing-jün</rt></ruby>。

<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>Wa-yiu</rt><rb>當該</rb><rt>tông-kæ</rt><rb>獻</rb><rt>hyin</rt><rb>一隻</rb><rt>ih-tsah</rt><rb>弗滿</rb><rt>feh-mun</rt><rb>一年</rb><rt>ih-nyin</rt><rb>雄小羊</rb><rt>yüong-siao-yiang</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>贖罪祭</rb><rt>joh-ze-tsi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>許願</rb><rt>hyü-nyün</rt><rb>誠心</rb><rt>dzing-sing</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>遭着</rb><rt>tsao-djoh</rt><rb>污穢</rb><rt>u-w̆e</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>以前</rb><rt>yi-zin</rt><rb>誠心個</rb><rt>dzing-sing-go</rt><rb>日子</rb><rt>nyih-ts</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>算數</rb><rt>sön-su</rt></ruby>。

<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>誠心個</rb><rt>dzing-sing-go</rt><rb>日子</rb><rt>nyih-ts</rt><rb>滿足</rb><rt>mun-tsoh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>常例</rb><rt>djông-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>帶</rb><rt>ta</rt><rb>許願個</rb><rt>hyü-nyün-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>會幕</rb><rt>we-mo</rt><rb>門口</rb><rt>meng-kʽeo</rt></ruby>。

<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>祭司</rb><rt>Tsi-s</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>該</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>獻上</rb><rt>hyin-zông</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>耶和華個</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô-go</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>獻上</rb><rt>hyin-zông</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>贖罪祭</rb><rt>joh-ze-tsi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>燒化祭</rb><rt>siao-hwô-tsi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>Yi</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>獻</rb><rt>hyin</rt><rb>箇隻</rb><rt>keh-tsah</rt><rb>雄羊</rb><rt>yüong-yiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>一籃</rb><rt>ih-læn</rt><rb>嘸酵水個</rb><rt>m-kao-shü-go</rt><rb>餅</rb><rt>ping</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>謝恩祭</rb><rt>zia-eng-tsi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>祭司</rb><rt>tsi-s</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>獻</rb><rt>hyin</rt><rb>配攏個</rb><rt>pʽe-long-go</rt><rb>素祭</rb><rt>su-tsi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>奠個</rb><rt>din-go</rt><rb>酒</rb><rt>tsiu</rt></ruby>。

<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>誠心個</rb><rt>Dzing-sing-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>應該</rb><rt>ing-kæ</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>會幕</rb><rt>we-mo</rt><rb>門口</rb><rt>meng-kʽeo</rt><rb>剃頭</rb><rt>tʽi-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>將</rb><rt>tsiang</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>頭髮</rb><rt>deo-fah</rt><rb>安來</rb><rt>en-læ</rt><rb>謝恩祭</rb><rt>zia-eng-tsi</rt><rb>下頭個</rb><rt>ʽô-deo-go</rt><rb>火裏</rb><rt>ho-li</rt></ruby>。

<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>祭司</rb><rt>Tsi-s</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>馱</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>煮熟了</rb><rt>ts-joh-liao</rt><rb>雄羊</rb><rt>yüong-yiang</rt><rb>一隻</rb><rt>ih-tsah</rt><rb>前腿</rb><rt>zin-tʽe</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>籃裏</rb><rt>læn-li</rt><rb>馱</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>嘸酵水個</rb><rt>m-kao-shü-go</rt><rb>餅</rb><rt>ping</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>安在</rb><rt>en-dzæ</rt><rb>剃頭過了</rb><rt>tʽi-deo-ko-liao</rt><rb>尊起</rb><rt>tseng-kyʽi</rt><rb>人個</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>手裏</rb><rt>siu-li</rt></ruby>。

<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>祭司</rb><rt>Tsi-s</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>耶和華個</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô-go</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>將</rb><rt>tsiang</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>禮物</rb><rt>li-veh</rt><rb>搖一搖</rb><rt>yiao-ih-yiao</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>搖個</rb><rt>yiao-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>舉上個</rb><rt>kyü-zông-go</rt><rb>腿</rb><rt>tʽe</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt><rb>誠心個</rb><rt>dzing-sing-go</rt><rb>歸撥</rb><rt>kwe-peh</rt><rb>祭司</rb><rt>tsi-s</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>以後</rb><rt>yi-ʽeo</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>尊起個</rb><rt>tseng-kyʽi-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>可以</rb><rt>kʽo-yi</rt><rb>喝酒</rb><rt>hah-tsiu</rt></ruby>。

<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>許願</rb><rt>hyü-nyün</rt><rb>尊起個</rb><rt>tseng-kyʽi-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>尊起個</rb><rt>tseng-kyʽi-go</rt><rb>日子</rb><rt>nyih-ts</rt><rb>滿足</rb><rt>mun-tsoh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>獻</rb><rt>hyin</rt><rb>祭物</rb><rt>tsi-veh</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>耶和華個</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô-go</rt><rb>常例</rb><rt>djông-li</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>力量</rb><rt>lih-liang</rt><rb>可以</rb><rt>kʽo-yi</rt><rb>另外</rb><rt>ling-nga</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>獻</rb><rt>hyin</rt><rb>祭物</rb><rt>tsi-veh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>該</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>許個</rb><rt>hyü-go</rt><rb>願</rb><rt>nyün</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>22</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：

<sup>23</sup> 「<ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>Yüong</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞倫</rb><rt>Üô-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>祝福</rb><rt>coh-foh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孫</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：

<sup>24</sup> 『<ruby><rb>但願</rb><rt>Dæn-nyün</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>賜福</rb><rt>s-foh</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>保護</rb><rt>pao-wu</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>；

<sup>25</sup> <ruby><rb>但願</rb><rt>Dæn-nyün</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>面容個</rb><rt>min-yüong-go</rt><rb>亮光</rb><rt>liang-kwông</rt><rb>照着</rb><rt>tsiao-djoh</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>賜</rb><rt>s</rt><rb>恩惠</rb><rt>eng-we</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>；

<sup>26</sup> <ruby><rb>但願</rb><rt>Dæn-nyün</rt><rb>耶和華個</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô-go</rt><rb>面容</rb><rt>min-yüong</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>向着</rb><rt>hyiang-djoh</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>賜落</rb><rt>s-lôh</rt><rb>平安</rb><rt>bing-en</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>。』

<sup>27</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>奉</rb><rt>vong</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>名頭</rb><rt>ming-deo</rt><rb>祝福</rb><rt>coh-foh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孫</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>賜福</rb><rt>s-foh</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>。」

### Ms. VII.

<u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>MO-SI</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>搭好</rb><rt>tah-hao</rt><rb>帳篷</rb><rt>tsiang-bong</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>油</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>傅</rb><rt>fu</rt><rb>帳篷</rb><rt>tsiang-bong</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>一切個</rb><rt>ih-tsʽih-go</rt><rb>物件</rb><rt>veh-gyin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>成爲</rb><rt>dzing-we</rt><rb>聖善</rb><rt>sing-jün</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>傅</rb><rt>fu</rt><rb>祭壇</rb><rt>tsi-dæn</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>一切個</rb><rt>ih-tsʽih-go</rt><rb>器皿</rb><rt>kyʽi-ming</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>成爲</rb><rt>dzing-we</rt><rb>聖善</rb><rt>sing-jün</rt></ruby>。

<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>當時</rb><rt>Tông-z</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>以色列</u>個</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih-go</rt><rb>衆</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>牧伯</rb><rt>moh-pah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>各</rb><rt>kôh</rt><rb>支派個</rb><rt>ts-pʽa-go</rt><rb>族長</rb><rt>dzoh-tsiang</rt><rb>獻</rb><rt>hyin</rt><rb>禮物</rb><rt>li-veh</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>都是</rb><rt>tu-z</rt><rb>支派個</rb><rt>ts-pʽa-go</rt><rb>牧伯</rb><rt>moh-pah</rt><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>管住</rb><rt>kwun-djü</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>數過了個</rb><rt>su-ko-liao-go</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt></ruby>。

<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>帶來個</rb><rt>ta-læ-go</rt><rb>禮物</rb><rt>li-veh</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>耶和華個</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô-go</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>篷個</rb><rt>bong-go</rt><rb>車子</rb><rt>tsʽô-ts</rt><rb>六乘</rb><rt>loh-dzing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>牛</rb><rt>ngeo</rt><rb>十二</rb><rt>jih-nyi</rt><rb>頭</rb><rt>deo</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>兩個</rb><rt>liang-go</rt><rb>牧伯</rb><rt>moh-pah</rt><rb>獻</rb><rt>hyin</rt><rb>一乘</rb><rt>ih-dzing</rt><rb>車子</rb><rt>tsʽô-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>各人</rb><rt>kôh-nying</rt><rb>一頭</rb><rt>ih-deo</rt><rb>牛</rb><rt>ngeo</rt><rb>送</rb><rt>song</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>帳篷</rb><rt>tsiang-bong</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt></ruby>。

<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：

<sup>5</sup> 「<ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>Yüong</rt><rb>接受</rb><rt>tsih-ziu</rt><rb>其拉個</rb><rt>gyi-lah-go</rt><rb>禮物</rb><rt>li-veh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>會幕個</rb><rt>we-mo-go</rt><rb>公用</rb><rt>kong-yüong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>交代</rb><rt>kao-dæ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>利未</rb><rt>Li-vi</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>各人</rb><rt>kôh-nying</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>當個</rb><rt>tông-go</rt><rb>差使</rb><rt>tsʽa-s</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>6</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>接受</rb><rt>tsih-ziu</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>六乘</rb><rt>loh-dzing</rt><rb>車子</rb><rt>tsʽô-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>十二</rb><rt>jih-nyi</rt><rb>頭</rb><rt>deo</rt><rb>牛</rb><rt>ngeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>交代</rb><rt>kao-dæ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>利未</rb><rt>Li-vi</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。

<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>將</rb><rt>Tsiang</rt><rb>兩乘</rb><rt>liang-dzing</rt><rb>車子</rb><rt>tsʽô-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>四頭</rb><rt>s-deo</rt><rb>牛</rb><rt>ngeo</rt><rb>交代</rb><rt>kao-dæ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>革順</rb><rt>Kah-jing</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孫</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>當個</rb><rt>tông-go</rt><rb>差使</rb><rt>tsʽa-s</rt></ruby>。

<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>將</rb><rt>Tsiang</rt><rb>四乘</rb><rt>s-dzing</rt><rb>車子</rb><rt>tsʽô-ts</rt><rb>八頭</rb><rt>pah-deo</rt><rb>牛</rb><rt>ngeo</rt><rb>交代</rb><rt>kao-dæ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>米拉利</rb><rt>Mi-lah-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孫</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>當個</rb><rt>tông-go</rt><rb>差使</rb><rt>tsʽa-s</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>都是</rb><rt>tu-z</rt><rb>祭司</rb><rt>tsi-s</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞倫</rb><rt>Üô-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以大瑪</rb><rt>Yi-da-mô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>掌管</rb><rt>công-kwun</rt></ruby>。

<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>只有</rb><rt>Tsih-yiu</rt><rb>嘸有</rb><rt>m-yiu</rt><rb>交代</rb><rt>kao-dæ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>哥轄</rb><rt>Ko-ʽeh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孫</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>聖所個</rb><rt>sing-sô-go</rt><rb>差使</rb><rt>tsʽa-s</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>歸</rb><rt>kwe</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都是</rb><rt>tu-z</rt><rb>肩胛</rb><rt>kyin-kah</rt><rb>頭</rb><rt>deo</rt><rb>抬個</rb><rt>dæ-go</rt></ruby>。

<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>Yüong</rt><rb>油</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>傅</rb><rt>fu</rt><rb>祭壇</rb><rt>tsi-dæn</rt><rb>箇日</rb><rt>keh-nyih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>衆</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>牧伯</rb><rt>moh-pah</rt><rb>獻</rb><rt>hyin</rt><rb>禮物</rb><rt>li-veh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>祭壇</rb><rt>tsi-dæn</rt><rb>行</rb><rt>ʽang</rt><rb>成功個</rb><rt>dzing-kong-go</rt><rb>禮</rb><rt>li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>帶</rb><rt>ta</rt><rb>其拉個</rb><rt>gyi-lah-go</rt><rb>禮物</rb><rt>li-veh</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>祭壇前</rb><rt>tsi-dæn-zin</rt></ruby>。

<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>行</rb><rt>ʽang</rt><rb>祭壇</rb><rt>tsi-dæn</rt><rb>成功個</rb><rt>dzing-kong-go</rt><rb>禮</rb><rt>li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>應該</rb><rt>ing-kæ</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>牧伯</rb><rt>moh-pah</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>一日</rb><rt>ih-nyih</rt><rb>獻上</rb><rt>hyin-zông</rt><rb>禮物</rb><rt>li-veh</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>第一</rb><rt>Di-ih</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>獻</rb><rt>hyin</rt><rb>禮物</rb><rt>li-veh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>猶大</rb><rt>Yiu-da</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>支派個</rb><rt>ts-pʽa-go</rt><rb>牧伯</rb><rt>moh-pah</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>亞米拿達</u>個</rb><rt>Üô-mi-nô-deh-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>拿順</rb><rt>Nô-jing</rt></ruby></u>。

<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>獻上個</rb><rt>hyin-zông-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>銀盆</rb><rt>nying-beng</rt><rb>一隻</rb><rt>ih-tsah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一百</rb><rt>ih-pah</rt><rb>三十</rb><rt>sæn-jih</rt><rb>㖷喀</rb><rt>z-kʽah</rt><rb>重</rb><rt>djong</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>銀碗</rb><rt>nying-un</rt><rb>一隻</rb><rt>ih-tsah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>七十</rb><rt>tsʽih-jih</rt><rb>㖷喀</rb><rt>z-kʽah</rt><rb>重</rb><rt>djong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>聖所個</rb><rt>sing-sô-go</rt><rb>天平</rb><rt>tʽin-bing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>油</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>和的個</rb><rt>ʽo-tih-go</rt><rb>細麪粉</rb><rt>si-min-feng</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>素祭</rb><rt>su-tsi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>𣥼滿了個</rb><rt>tsi-mun-liao-go</rt></ruby>。

<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>Wa-yiu</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>香</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>𣥼滿個</rb><rt>tsi-mun-go</rt><rb>金爐</rb><rt>kying-lu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>十個</rb><rt>jih-go</rt><rb>㖷喀</rb><rt>z-kʽah</rt><rb>重</rb><rt>djong</rt></ruby>：

<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>小</rb><rt>Siao</rt><rb>雄牛</rb><rt>yüong-ngeo</rt><rb>一頭</rb><rt>ih-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>雄</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>湖羊</rb><rt>wu-yiang</rt><rb>一隻</rb><rt>ih-tsah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗滿</rb><rt>feh-mun</rt><rb>一年個</rb><rt>ih-nyin-go</rt><rb>雄</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>小羊</rb><rt>siao-yiang</rt><rb>一隻</rb><rt>ih-tsah</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>燒化祭</rb><rt>siao-hwô-tsi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>雄</rb><rt>Yüong</rt><rb>山羊</rb><rt>sæn-yiang</rt><rb>一隻</rb><rt>ih-tsah</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>贖罪祭</rb><rt>joh-ze-tsi</rt></ruby>；

<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>牛</rb><rt>Ngeo</rt><rb>兩頭</rb><rt>liang-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>雄</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>湖羊</rb><rt>wu-yiang</rt><rb>五隻</rb><rt>ng-tsah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>雄</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>山羊</rb><rt>sæn-yiang</rt><rb>五隻</rb><rt>ng-tsah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗滿</rb><rt>feh-mun</rt><rb>一年個</rb><rt>ih-nyin-go</rt><rb>雄</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>小羊</rb><rt>siao-yiang</rt><rb>五隻</rb><rt>ng-tsah</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>謝恩祭</rb><rt>zia-eng-tsi</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞米拿達</rb><rt>Üô-mi-nô-deh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>拿順</rb><rt>Nô-jing</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>獻個</rb><rt>hyin-go</rt></ruby>。

<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>第二日</rb><rt>Di-nyi-nyih</rt><rb>獻</rb><rt>hyin</rt><rb>禮物個</rb><rt>li-veh-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>以薩迦</u>個</rb><rt>Yi-sah-kyüô-go</rt><rb>牧伯</rb><rt>moh-pah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb><u>蘇押</u>個</rb><rt>Su-iah-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>拿但業</rb><rt>Nô-dæn-nyih</rt></ruby></u>。

<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>獻個</rb><rt>hyin-go</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>銀盆</rb><rt>nying-beng</rt><rb>一隻</rb><rt>ih-tsah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一百</rb><rt>ih-pah</rt><rb>三十</rb><rt>sæn-jih</rt><rb>㖷喀</rb><rt>z-kʽah</rt><rb>重</rb><rt>djong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>銀碗</rb><rt>nying-un</rt><rb>一隻</rb><rt>ih-tsah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>七十</rb><rt>tsʽih-jih</rt><rb>㖷喀</rb><rt>z-kʽah</rt><rb>重</rb><rt>djong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>聖所個</rb><rt>sing-sô-go</rt><rb>天平</rb><rt>tʽin-bing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>油</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>和的個</rb><rt>ʽo-tih-go</rt><rb>細麪粉</rb><rt>si-min-feng</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>素祭</rb><rt>su-tsi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>𣥼滿了個</rb><rt>tsi-mun-liao-go</rt></ruby>。

<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>小</rb><rt>Siao</rt><rb>雄牛</rb><rt>yüong-ngeo</rt><rb>一頭</rb><rt>ih-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>雄</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>湖羊</rb><rt>wu-yiang</rt><rb>一隻</rb><rt>ih-tsah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗滿</rb><rt>feh-mun</rt><rb>一年個</rb><rt>ih-nyin-go</rt><rb>雄</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>小羊</rb><rt>siao-yiang</rt><rb>一隻</rb><rt>ih-tsah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>燒化祭</rb><rt>siao-hwô-tsi</rt></ruby>；

<sup>22</sup> <ruby><rb>雄</rb><rt>Yüong</rt><rb>山羊</rb><rt>sæn-yiang</rt><rb>一隻</rb><rt>ih-tsah</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>贖罪祭</rb><rt>joh-ze-tsi</rt></ruby>；

<sup>23</sup> <ruby><rb>牛</rb><rt>Ngeo</rt><rb>兩頭</rb><rt>liang-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>雄</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>湖羊</rb><rt>wu-yiang</rt><rb>五隻</rb><rt>ng-tsah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>雄</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>山羊</rb><rt>sæn-yiang</rt><rb>五隻</rb><rt>ng-tsah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗滿</rb><rt>feh-mun</rt><rb>一年個</rb><rt>ih-nyin-go</rt><rb>雄</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>小羊</rb><rt>siao-yiang</rt><rb>五隻</rb><rt>ng-tsah</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>謝恩祭</rb><rt>zia-eng-tsi</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>蘇押</u>個</rb><rt>Su-iah-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>拿但業</rb><rt>Nô-dæn-nyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>獻個</rb><rt>hyin-go</rt></ruby>。

<sup>24</sup> <ruby><rb>第三日</rb><rt>Di-sæn-nyih</rt><rb>獻</rb><rt>hyin</rt><rb>禮物個</rb><rt>li-veh-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>西布倫</rb><rt>Si-pu-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孫個</rb><rt>ts-seng-go</rt><rb>牧伯</rb><rt>moh-pah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb><u>希倫</u>個</rb><rt>Hyi-leng-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以利押</rb><rt>Yi-li-iah</rt></ruby></u>。

<sup>25</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>獻個</rb><rt>hyin-go</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>銀盆</rb><rt>nying-beng</rt><rb>一隻</rb><rt>ih-tsah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一百</rb><rt>ih-pah</rt><rb>三十</rb><rt>sæn-jih</rt><rb>㖷喀</rb><rt>z-kʽah</rt><rb>重</rb><rt>djong</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>銀碗</rb><rt>nying-un</rt><rb>一隻</rb><rt>ih-tsah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>七十</rb><rt>tsʽih-jih</rt><rb>㖷喀</rb><rt>z-kʽah</rt><rb>重</rb><rt>djong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>聖所個</rb><rt>sing-sô-go</rt><rb>天平</rb><rt>tʽin-bing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>油</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>和的個</rb><rt>ʽo-tih-go</rt><rb>細麪粉</rb><rt>si-min-feng</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>素祭</rb><rt>su-tsi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>𣥼滿了個</rb><rt>tsi-mun-liao-go</rt></ruby>。

<sup>26</sup> <ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>Wa-yiu</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>香</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>𣥼滿個</rb><rt>tsi-mun-go</rt><rb>金爐</rb><rt>kying-lu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>十個</rb><rt>jih-go</rt><rb>㖷喀</rb><rt>z-kʽah</rt><rb>重</rb><rt>djong</rt></ruby>。

<sup>79</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>獻個</rb><rt>hyin-go</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>銀盆</rb><rt>nying-beng</rt><rb>一隻</rb><rt>ih-tsah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一百</rb><rt>ih-pah</rt><rb>三十</rb><rt>sæn-jih</rt><rb>㖷喀</rb><rt>z-kʽah</rt><rb>重</rb><rt>djong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>銀碗</rb><rt>nying-un</rt><rb>一隻</rb><rt>ih-tsah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>七十</rb><rt>tsʽih-jih</rt><rb>㖷喀</rb><rt>z-kʽah</rt><rb>重</rb><rt>djong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>聖所個</rb><rt>sing-sô-go</rt><rb>天平</rb><rt>tʽin-bing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>油</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>和的個</rb><rt>ʽo-tih-go</rt><rb>細麪粉</rb><rt>si-min-feng</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>素祭</rb><rt>su-tsi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>𣥼滿了個</rb><rt>tsi-mun-liao-go</rt></ruby>。

<sup>80</sup> <ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>Wa-yiu</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>香</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>𣥼滿個</rb><rt>tsi-mun-go</rt><rb>金爐</rb><rt>kying-lu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>十個</rb><rt>jih-go</rt><rb>㖷喀</rb><rt>z-kʽah</rt><rb>重</rb><rt>djong</rt></ruby>。

<sup>81</sup> <ruby><rb>小</rb><rt>Siao</rt><rb>雄牛</rb><rt>yüong-ngeo</rt><rb>一頭</rb><rt>ih-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>雄</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>湖羊</rb><rt>wu-yiang</rt><rb>一隻</rb><rt>ih-tsah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗滿</rb><rt>feh-mun</rt><rb>一年個</rb><rt>ih-nyin-go</rt><rb>雄</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>小羊</rb><rt>siao-yiang</rt><rb>一隻</rb><rt>ih-tsah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>燒化祭</rb><rt>siao-hwô-tsi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>82</sup> <ruby><rb>雄</rb><rt>Yüong</rt><rb>山羊</rb><rt>sæn-yiang</rt><rb>一隻</rb><rt>ih-tsah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>贖罪祭</rb><rt>joh-ze-tsi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>83</sup> <ruby><rb>牛</rb><rt>Ngeo</rt><rb>兩頭</rb><rt>liang-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>雄</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>湖羊</rb><rt>wu-yiang</rt><rb>五隻</rb><rt>ng-tsah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>雄</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>山羊</rb><rt>sæn-yiang</rt><rb>五隻</rb><rt>ng-tsah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗滿</rb><rt>feh-mun</rt><rb>一年個</rb><rt>ih-nyin-go</rt><rb>雄</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>小羊</rb><rt>siao-yiang</rt><rb>五隻</rb><rt>ng-tsah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>謝恩祭</rb><rt>zia-eng-tsi</rt></ruby>：<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>以南</u>個</rb><rt>Yi-nen-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞希拉</rb><rt>Üô-hyi-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>獻個</rb><rt>hyin-go</rt></ruby>。

<sup>84</sup> <ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>Yüong</rt><rb>油</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>傅</rb><rt>fu</rt><rb>祭壇</rb><rt>tsi-dæn</rt><rb>箇日</rb><rt>keh-nyih</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>衆</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>牧伯</rb><rt>moh-pah</rt><rb>行</rb><rt>ʽang</rt><rb>成功個</rb><rt>dzing-kong-go</rt><rb>禮</rb><rt>li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>獻個</rb><rt>hyin-go</rt><rb>禮物</rb><rt>li-veh</rt><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>十二</rb><rt>jih-nyi</rt><rb>隻</rb><rt>tsah</rt><rb>銀盆</rb><rt>nying-beng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>十二</rb><rt>jih-nyi</rt><rb>隻</rb><rt>tsah</rt><rb>銀碗</rb><rt>nying-un</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>十二個</rb><rt>jih-nyi-go</rt><rb>金爐</rb><rt>kying-lu</rt></ruby>；

<sup>85</sup> <ruby><rb>每隻</rb><rt>Me-tsah</rt><rb>銀盆</rb><rt>nying-beng</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一百</rb><rt>ih-pah</rt><rb>三十</rb><rt>sæn-jih</rt><rb>㖷喀</rb><rt>z-kʽah</rt><rb>重</rb><rt>djong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>每隻</rb><rt>me-tsah</rt><rb>銀碗</rb><rt>nying-un</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>七十</rb><rt>tsʽih-jih</rt><rb>㖷喀</rb><rt>z-kʽah</rt><rb>重</rb><rt>djong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>器皿個</rb><rt>kyʽi-ming-go</rt><rb>銀子</rb><rt>nying-ts</rt><rb>共</rb><rt>gong</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>二千</rb><rt>nyi-tsʽin</rt><rb>四百</rb><rt>s-pah</rt><rb>㖷喀</rb><rt>z-kʽah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>聖所個</rb><rt>sing-sô-go</rt><rb>天平</rb><rt>tʽin-bing</rt></ruby>。

<sup>86</sup> <ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>十二個</rb><rt>jih-nyi-go</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>香</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>𣥼滿個</rb><rt>tsi-mun-go</rt><rb>金爐</rb><rt>kying-lu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>每</rb><rt>me</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>十個</rb><rt>jih-go</rt><rb>㖷喀</rb><rt>z-kʽah</rt><rb>重</rb><rt>djong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>聖所個</rb><rt>sing-sô-go</rt><rb>天平</rb><rt>tʽin-bing</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>爐個</rb><rt>lu-go</rt><rb>金子</rb><rt>kying-ts</rt><rb>共</rb><rt>gong</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一百</rb><rt>ih-pah</rt><rb>廿</rb><rt>nyiæn</rt><rb>㖷喀</rb><rt>z-kʽah</rt></ruby>。

<sup>87</sup> <ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>Tso</rt><rb>燒化祭個</rb><rt>siao-hwô-tsi-go</rt><rb>牲口</rb><rt>sang-kʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>小牛</rb><rt>siao-ngeo</rt><rb>十二頭</rb><rt>jih-nyi-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>雄</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>湖羊</rb><rt>wu-yiang</rt><rb>十二</rb><rt>jih-nyi</rt><rb>隻</rb><rt>tsah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗滿</rb><rt>feh-mun</rt><rb>一年個</rb><rt>ih-nyin-go</rt><rb>雄</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>小羊</rb><rt>siao-yiang</rt><rb>十二</rb><rt>jih-nyi</rt><rb>隻</rb><rt>tsah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>獻個</rb><rt>hyin-go</rt><rb>素祭</rb><rt>su-tsi</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>贖罪祭個</rb><rt>joh-ze-tsi-go</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>雄</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>山羊</rb><rt>sæn-yiang</rt><rb>十二</rb><rt>jih-nyi</rt><rb>隻</rb><rt>tsah</rt></ruby>。

<sup>88</sup> <ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>Tso</rt><rb>謝恩祭</rb><rt>zia-eng-tsi</rt><rb>共</rb><rt>gong</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>雄牛</rb><rt>yüong-ngeo</rt><rb>廿四</rb><rt>nyiæn-s</rt><rb>頭</rb><rt>deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>雄</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>湖羊</rb><rt>wu-yiang</rt><rb>六十</rb><rt>loh-jih</rt><rb>隻</rb><rt>tsah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>雄</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>山羊</rb><rt>sæn-yiang</rt><rb>六十</rb><rt>loh-jih</rt><rb>隻</rb><rt>tsah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗滿</rb><rt>feh-mun</rt><rb>一年個</rb><rt>ih-nyin-go</rt><rb>雄</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>小羊</rb><rt>siao-yiang</rt><rb>六十</rb><rt>loh-jih</rt><rb>隻</rb><rt>tsah</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>油</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>傅</rb><rt>fu</rt><rb>祭壇</rb><rt>tsi-dæn</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>行</rb><rt>ʽang</rt><rb>成功個</rb><rt>dzing-kong-go</rt><rb>禮</rb><rt>li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>獻上個</rb><rt>hyin-zông-go</rt><rb>禮物</rb><rt>li-veh</rt></ruby>。

<sup>89</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>走進</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>會幕裏</rb><rt>we-mo-li</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>keh-tsao</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>聽聞</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>聲音</rb><rt>sing-ing</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>法櫃</rb><rt>fah-gwe</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>贖罪</rb><rt>joh-ze</rt><rb>臺上</rb><rt>dæ-zông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>兩</rb><rt>liang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基路冰</rb><rt>Kyi-lu-ping</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>出來</rb><rt>cʽih-læ</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>聲音</rb><rt>sing-ing</rt><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt></ruby>。

### Ms. VIII.

<ruby><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>YIÆ-ʽO-WÔ</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：

<sup>2</sup> 「<ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>Yüong</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞倫</rb><rt>Üô-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>點燈</rb><rt>tin-teng</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>必要</rb><rt>pih-iao</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>七盞燈</rb><rt>tsʽih-tsæn-teng</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>燈臺</rb><rt>teng-dæ</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>發光</rb><rt>fah-kwông</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>3</sup> <u><ruby><rb>亞倫</rb><rt>Üô-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>點燈</rb><rt>tin-teng</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>燈臺</rb><rt>teng-dæ</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>。

<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>燈臺個</rb><rt>Teng-dæ-go</rt><rb>做法</rb><rt>tso-fah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>打鍊個</rb><rt>tang-lin-go</rt><rb>金子</rb><rt>kying-ts</rt><rb>鑄造</rb><rt>cü-zao</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>座盤</rb><rt>zo-bun</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>稈子</rb><rt>ken-ts</rt><rb>都是</rb><rt>tu-z</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>打鍊個</rb><rt>tang-lin-go</rt><rb>生活</rb><rt>sang-weh</rt><rb>鑄造個</rb><rt>cü-zao-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>現出</rb><rt>yin-cʽih</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>看個</rb><rt>kʽen-go</rt><rb>樣式</rb><rt>yiang-shih</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>。

<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：

<sup>6</sup> 「<ruby><rb>將</rb><rt>Tsiang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>利未</rb><rt>Li-vi</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孫</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>分出來</rb><rt>feng-cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>潔淨</rb><rt>kyih-zing</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>。

<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>Iao</rt><rb>潔淨</rb><rt>kyih-zing</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>應該</rb><rt>ing-kæ</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>贖罪</rb><rt>joh-ze</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>扎扎</rb><rt>tsah-tsah</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>身體</rb><rt>sing-tʽi</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>刀</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>剃</rb><rt>tʽi</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>全體</rb><rt>djün-tʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>滰</rb><rt>gyiang</rt><rb>衣裳</rb><rt>i-zông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>潔淨</rb><rt>kyih-zing</rt></ruby>。

<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>Wa-yiu</rt><rb>應該</rb><rt>ing-kæ</rt><rb>馱</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>一頭</rb><rt>ih-deo</rt><rb>小</rb><rt>siao</rt><rb>雄牛</rb><rt>yüong-ngeo</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>油</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>和的個</rb><rt>ʽo-tih-go</rt><rb>細麪粉</rb><rt>si-min-feng</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>配攏個</rb><rt>pʽe-long-go</rt><rb>素祭</rb><rt>su-tsi</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>馱</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>一頭</rb><rt>ih-deo</rt><rb>雄牛</rb><rt>yüong-ngeo</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>贖罪祭</rb><rt>joh-ze-tsi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>帶</rb><rt>ta</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>利未</rb><rt>Li-vi</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>會幕</rb><rt>we-mo</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>召攏</rb><rt>dziao-long</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孫個</rb><rt>ts-seng-go</rt><rb>衆</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>公會</rb><rt>kong-we</rt></ruby>。

<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>帶</rb><rt>ta</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>利未</rb><rt>Li-vi</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孫</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>該</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>按手</rb><rt>en-siu</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>利未</rb><rt>Li-vi</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>上頭</rb><rt>zông-deo</rt></ruby>。

<sup>11</sup> <u><ruby><rb>亞倫</rb><rt>Üô-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>該</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>將</rb><rt>tsiang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>利未</rb><rt>Li-vi</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>獻</rb><rt>hyin</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>代</rb><rt>dæ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孫</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>公獻個</rb><rt>kong-hyin-go</rt><rb>禮物</rb><rt>li-veh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>當</rb><rt>tông</rt><rb>耶和華個</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô-go</rt><rb>差使</rb><rt>tsʽa-s</rt></ruby>。

<sup>12</sup> <u><ruby><rb>利未</rb><rt>Li-vi</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>應該</rb><rt>ing-kæ</rt><rb>按手</rb><rt>en-siu</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>雄牛個</rb><rt>yüong-ngeo-go</rt><rb>頭上</rb><rt>deo-zông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>後頭</rb><rt>ʽeo-deo</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>該</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>將</rb><rt>tsiang</rt><rb>牛</rb><rt>ngeo</rt><rb>一頭</rb><rt>ih-deo</rt><rb>獻撥</rb><rt>hyin-peh</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>贖罪祭</rb><rt>joh-ze-tsi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一頭</rb><rt>ih-deo</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>燒化</rb><rt>siao-hwô</rt><rb>祭</rb><rt>tsi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>替</rb><rt>tʽi</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>利未</rb><rt>Li-vi</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>贖罪</rb><rt>joh-ze</rt></ruby>。

<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>該</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>將</rb><rt>tsiang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>利未</rb><rt>Li-vi</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>立</rb><rt>lih</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞倫</rb><rt>Üô-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>並且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>將</rb><rt>tsiang</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>當</rb><rt>tông</rt><rb>獻</rb><rt>hyin</rt><rb>公個</rb><rt>kong-go</rt><rb>禮物</rb><rt>li-veh</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt></ruby>。

<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>Z-ka</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>將</rb><rt>tsiang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>利未</rb><rt>Li-vi</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孫個</rb><rt>ts-seng-go</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>分別</rb><rt>feng-pih</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>利未</rb><rt>Li-vi</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>歸撥</rb><rt>kwe-peh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>。

<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>必要</rb><rt>pih-iao</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>潔淨</rb><rt>kyih-zing</rt><rb>身體</rb><rt>sing-tʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>將</rb><rt>tsiang</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>獻上</rb><rt>hyin-zông</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>後頭</rb><rt>ʽeo-deo</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>可以</rb><rt>kʽo-yi</rt><rb>走進</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>會幕裏</rb><rt>we-mo-li</rt><rb>當</rb><rt>tông</rt><rb>差使</rb><rt>tsʽa-s</rt></ruby>。

<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孫</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>全全</rb><rt>djün-djün</rt><rb>歸撥</rb><rt>kwe-peh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>揀選</rb><rt>kæn-shün</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>歸撥</rb><rt>kwe-peh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>替代</rb><rt>tʽi-dæ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>一切</rb><rt>ih-tsʽih</rt><rb>頭生個</rb><rt>deo-sang-go</rt></ruby>。

<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>攏總</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>頭生個</rb><rt>deo-sang-go</rt><rb>弗論</rb><rt>feh-leng</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗論</rb><rt>feh-leng</rt><rb>衆生</rb><rt>cong-sang</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>ngô-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>打煞</rb><rt>tang-sah</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地個</rb><rt>di-go</rt><rb>一切</rb><rt>ih-tsʽih</rt><rb>頭生個</rb><rt>deo-sang-go</rt><rb>箇日</rb><rt>keh-nyih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>尊起</rb><rt>tseng-kyʽi</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>歸撥</rb><rt>kwe-peh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>。

<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>揀選</rb><rt>kæn-shün</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>利未</rb><rt>Li-vi</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>替代</rb><rt>tʽi-dæ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孫</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>一切</rb><rt>ih-tsʽih</rt><rb>頭生個</rb><rt>deo-sang-go</rt></ruby>。

<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孫</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>將</rb><rt>tsiang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>利未</rb><rt>Li-vi</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>賜撥</rb><rt>s-peh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞倫</rb><rt>Üô-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>替</rb><rt>tʽi</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孫</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>當</rb><rt>tông</rt><rb>會幕個</rb><rt>we-mo-go</rt><rb>差使</rb><rt>tsʽa-s</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>以色列</u>個</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih-go</rt><rb>子孫</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>贖罪</rb><rt>joh-ze</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孫</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>近攏</rb><rt>gying-long</rt><rb>聖所</rb><rt>Sing-sô</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>遭着</rb><rt>tsao-djoh</rt><rb>禍祟</rb><rt>ʽo-se</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>20</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>亞倫</rb><rt>Üô-leng</rt></ruby></u>、<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>衆</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>公會</rb><rt>kong-we</rt><rb>向着</rb><rt>hyiang-djoh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>利未</rb><rt>Li-vi</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>講到</rb><rt>kông-tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>利未</rb><rt>Li-vi</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>摩西</u>個</rb><rt>Mo-si-go</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孫</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>待</rb><rt>dæ</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>。

<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>於是</rb><rt>Ü-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>利未</rb><rt>Li-vi</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>罪惡</rb><rt>ze-ôh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>滰</rb><rt>gyiang</rt><rb>潔淨</rb><rt>kyih-zing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>衣裳</rb><rt>i-zông</rt><rb>滰</rb><rt>gyiang</rt><rb>乾淨</rb><rt>ken-zing</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>亞倫</rb><rt>Üô-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>將</rb><rt>tsiang</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>獻撥</rb><rt>hyin-peh</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>替</rb><rt>tʽi</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>贖罪</rb><rt>joh-ze</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>潔淨</rb><rt>kyih-zing</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>。

<sup>22</sup> <ruby><rb>後頭</rb><rt>ʽEo-deo</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>利未</rb><rt>Li-vi</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>走進</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>會幕裏</rb><rt>we-mo-li</rt><rb>當</rb><rt>tông</rt><rb>差使</rb><rt>tsʽa-s</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞倫</rb><rt>Üô-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>講到</rb><rt>kông-tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>利未</rb><rt>Li-vi</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>所吩咐</rb><rt>sô-feng-fu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>向着</rb><rt>hyiang-djoh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>。

<sup>24</sup> <u><ruby><rb>利未</rb><rt>Li-vi</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>當</rb><rt>tông</rt><rb>差使</rb><rt>tsʽa-s</rt><rb>是介個</rb><rt>z-ka-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>廿五歲</rb><rt>nyiæn-ng-shü</rt><rb>起</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>該</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>走進</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>當</rb><rt>tông</rt><rb>會幕裏個</rb><rt>we-mo-li-go</rt><rb>差使</rb><rt>tsʽa-s</rt></ruby>。

<sup>25</sup> <ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>Tao</rt><rb>五十歲</rb><rt>ng-jih-shü</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>完畢</rb><rt>wun-pih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗再</rb><rt>feh-tsæ</rt><rb>當</rb><rt>tông</rt><rb>差使</rb><rt>tsʽa-s</rt></ruby>。

<sup>26</sup> <ruby><rb>可以</rb><rt>Kʽo-yi</rt><rb>幫助</rb><rt>pông-dzu</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt><rb>管守</rb><rt>kwun-siu</rt><rb>會幕</rb><rt>we-mo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>不必</rb><rt>peh-pih</rt><rb>當</rb><rt>tông</rt><rb>別個</rb><rt>bih-go</rt><rb>差使</rb><rt>tsʽa-s</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>派</rb><rt>pʽa</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>利未</rb><rt>Li-vi</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>當</rb><rt>tông</rt><rb>差使</rb><rt>tsʽa-s</rt><rb>應該</rb><rt>ing-kæ</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>待</rb><rt>dæ</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>。

### Ms. IX.

<u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>YI-SEH-LIH</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孫</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>出</rb><rt>cʽih</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>之後</rb><rt>ts-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>第二年</rb><rt>di-nyi-nyin</rt><rb>正月</rb><rt>tsing-yüih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>西難</rb><rt>Si-næn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>曠野</rb><rt>kwʽông-iæ</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：

<sup>2</sup> 「<u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孫</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>應該</rb><rt>ing-kæ</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>定個</rb><rt>ding-go</rt><rb>日子</rb><rt>nyih-ts</rt><rb>守</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>逾越節</rb><rt>yü-yüih-tsih</rt></ruby>。

<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>Ziu-z</rt><rb>本月</rb><rt>peng-yüih</rt><rb>十四</rb><rt>jih-s</rt><rb>箇日</rb><rt>keh-nyih</rt><rb>晚上</rb><rt>mæn-zông</rt><rb>應該</rb><rt>ing-kæ</rt><rb>守</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>節肯</rb><rt>tsih-kʽeng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>守</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>節肯</rb><rt>tsih-kʽeng</rt><rb>必要</rb><rt>pih-iao</rt><rb>保守</rb><rt>pao-siu</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>定好個</rb><rt>ding-hao-go</rt><rb>禮儀</rb><rt>li-nyi</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>於是</rb><rt>Ü-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孫</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>守</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>逾越節</rb><rt>yü-yüih-tsih</rt></ruby>。

<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>西難</rb><rt>Si-næn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>曠野</rb><rt>kwʽông-iæ</rt><rb>正月</rb><rt>tsing-yüih</rt><rb>十四</rb><rt>jih-s</rt><rb>箇日</rb><rt>keh-nyih</rt><rb>晚上</rb><rt>mæn-zông</rt><rb>守</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>逾越節</rb><rt>yü-yüih-tsih</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孫</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>。

<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>幾個</rb><rt>kyi-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>碰着</rb><rt>bang-djoh</rt><rb>屍首</rb><rt>s-siu</rt><rb>遭着</rb><rt>tsao-djoh</rt><rb>污穢</rb><rt>u-w̆e</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>能夠</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>當日</rb><rt>tông-nyih</rt><rb>守逾越節</rb><rt>siu-yü-yüih-tsih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇日</rb><rt>keh-nyih</rt><rb>走到</rb><rt>tseo-tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞倫</rb><rt>Üô-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt></ruby>，

<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>屍首</rb><rt>s-siu</rt><rb>遭着</rb><rt>tsao-djoh</rt><rb>污穢</rb><rt>u-w̆e</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>咋會</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>能夠</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孫</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>定規</rb><rt>ding-kwe</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>獻</rb><rt>hyin</rt><rb>耶和華個</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô-go</rt><rb>禮物</rb><rt>li-veh</rt></ruby>？」

<sup>8</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>等到</rb><rt>teng-tao</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>聽</rb><rt>tʽing</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>吩咐個</rb><rt>feng-fu-go</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：

<sup>10</sup> 「<ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>Yüong</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孫</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>爾拉個</rb><rt>ng-lah-go</rt><rb>後代</rb><rt>ʽeo-dæ</rt><rb>若有</rb><rt>ziah-yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>屍首個</rb><rt>s-siu-go</rt><rb>緣故</rb><rt>yün-kwu</rt><rb>遭着</rb><rt>tsao-djoh</rt><rb>污穢</rb><rt>u-w̆e</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽoh-tsia</rt><rb>出門</rb><rt>cʽih-meng</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>遠</rb><rt>yün</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>應該</rb><rt>ing-kæ</rt><rb>守</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>耶和華個</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô-go</rt><rb>逾越節</rb><rt>yü-yüih-tsih</rt></ruby>。

<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>Læ</rt><rb>二月</rb><rt>nyi-yüih</rt><rb>十四</rb><rt>jih-s</rt><rb>箇日</rb><rt>keh-nyih</rt><rb>晚上</rb><rt>mæn-zông</rt><rb>該</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>守個</rb><rt>siu-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>該</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>將</rb><rt>tsiang</rt><rb>無酵餅</rb><rt>vu-kao-ping</rt><rb>苦菜</rb><rt>kwʽu-tsʽæ</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>小羊</rb><rt>siao-yiang</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>喫</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt></ruby>。

<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>弗可</rb><rt>Feh-kʽo</rt><rb>留</rb><rt>liu</rt><rb>喫剩個</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-dzing-go</rt><rb>肉</rb><rt>nyüoh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>骨頭</rb><rt>kweh-deo</rt><rb>一根</rb><rt>ih-keng</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>弗可</rb><rt>feh-kʽo</rt><rb>拗斷</rb><rt>ao-dön</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>逾越節</rb><rt>yü-yüih-tsih</rt><rb>一切個</rb><rt>ih-tsʽih-go</rt><rb>規條</rb><rt>kwe-diao</rt><rb>守</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>節肯</rb><rt>tsih-kʽeng</rt></ruby>。

<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>不過</rb><rt>Peh-ko</rt><rb>潔淨</rb><rt>kyih-zing</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>弗出門個</rb><rt>feh-cʽih-meng-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>弗守</rb><rt>feh-siu</rt><rb>逾越節</rb><rt>yü-yüih-tsih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>必要</rb><rt>pih-iao</rt><rb>除滅</rb><rt>djü-mih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>定規個</rb><rt>ding-kwe-go</rt><rb>日子</rb><rt>nyih-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>獻</rb><rt>hyin</rt><rb>耶和華個</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô-go</rt><rb>祭物</rb><rt>tsi-veh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>必要</rb><rt>pih-iao</rt><rb>擔當</rb><rt>tæn-tông</rt><rb>其自個</rb><rt>gyi-zi-go</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt></ruby>。

<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>若有</rb><rt>Ziah-yiu</rt><rb>外頭</rb><rt>nga-deo</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>耽擱</rb><rt>tæn-koh</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>守</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>逾越節</rb><rt>yü-yüih-tsih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>應該</rb><rt>ing-kæ</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>逾越節個</rb><rt>yü-yüih-tsih-go</rt><rb>規條</rb><rt>kwe-diao</rt><rb>禮儀</rb><rt>li-nyi</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>外頭</rb><rt>nga-deo</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>本地</rb><rt>peng-di</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>一例個</rb><rt>ih-li-go</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>搭</rb><rt>Tah</rt><rb>帳篷</rb><rt>tsiang-bong</rt><rb>箇日</rb><rt>keh-nyih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>雲</rb><rt>yüing</rt><rb>蓋住</rb><rt>kæ-djü</rt><rb>帳篷</rb><rt>tsiang-bong</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>法幕</rb><rt>fah-mo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>夜快</rb><rt>yia-kwʽa</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>天亮</rb><rt>tʽin-liang</rt><rb>箇朵</rb><rt>keh-tô</rt><rb>雲</rb><rt>yüing</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>帳篷上</rb><rt>tsiang-bong-zông</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>光</rb><rt>kwông</rt><rb>現出來</rb><rt>yin-cʽih-læ</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>火</rb><rt>ho</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>。

<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>Ziu</rt><rb>常常</rb><rt>djông-djông</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>雲</rb><rt>yüing</rt><rb>蓋住</rb><rt>kæ-djü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>夜頭</rb><rt>yia-deo</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>火</rb><rt>ho</rt><rb>現出來</rb><rt>yin-cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>。

<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>雲</rb><rt>Yüing</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>帳篷上</rb><rt>tsiang-bong-zông</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>收去</rb><rt>siu-kyʽi</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>keh-tsao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孫</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>拔營</rb><rt>bah-ying</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>雲</rb><rt>yüing</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>啥個</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>庉落</rb><rt>deng-lôh</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孫</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>啥個</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>搭營</rb><rt>tah-ying</rt></ruby>。

<sup>18</sup> <u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孫</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>奉</rb><rt>vong</rt><rb>耶和華個</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô-go</rt><rb>命令</rb><rt>ming-ling</rt><rb>拔營</rb><rt>bah-ying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>奉</rb><rt>vong</rt><rb>耶和華個</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô-go</rt><rb>命令</rb><rt>ming-ling</rt><rb>搭營</rb><rt>tah-ying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>雲</rb><rt>yüing</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>帳篷上</rb><rt>tsiang-bong-zông</rt><rb>多少</rb><rt>to-siao</rt><rb>日子</rb><rt>nyih-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>營寨</rb><rt>ying-dza</rt><rb>庉住</rb><rt>deng-djü</rt><rb>多少</rb><rt>to-siao</rt><rb>日子</rb><rt>nyih-ts</rt></ruby>。

<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>雲</rb><rt>Yüing</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>帳篷上</rb><rt>tsiang-bong-zông</rt><rb>許多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>日子</rb><rt>nyih-ts</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孫</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>保守</rb><rt>pao-siu</rt><rb>耶和華個</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô-go</rt><rb>命令</rb><rt>ming-ling</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>拔</rb><rt>bah</rt><rb>營寨</rb><rt>ying-dza</rt></ruby>。

<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>雲</rb><rt>yüing</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>帳篷上</rb><rt>tsiang-bong-zông</rt><rb>幾日</rb><rt>kyi-nyih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>keh-tsao</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>耶和華個</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô-go</rt><rb>命令</rb><rt>ming-ling</rt><rb>庉</rb><rt>deng</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>營寨</rb><rt>ying-dza</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>耶和華個</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô-go</rt><rb>命令</rb><rt>ming-ling</rt><rb>拔營</rb><rt>bah-ying</rt></ruby>。

<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>雲</rb><rt>yüing</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>夜快</rb><rt>yia-kwʽa</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>天亮</rb><rt>tʽin-liang</rt><rb>庉落</rb><rt>deng-lôh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>雲</rb><rt>yüing</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>天亮</rb><rt>tʽin-liang</rt><rb>上去</rb><rt>zông-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>拔營</rb><rt>bah-ying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽoh-tsia</rt><rb>雲</rb><rt>yüing</rt><rb>庉</rb><rt>deng</rt><rb>一日</rb><rt>ih-nyih</rt><rb>一夜</rb><rt>ih-yia</rt><rb>上去</rb><rt>zông-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>拔營</rb><rt>bah-ying</rt></ruby>。

<sup>22</sup> <ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽÔh-tsia</rt><rb>兩日</rb><rt>liang-nyih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽoh-tsia</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-ko</rt><rb>月</rb><rt>yüih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽoh-tsia</rt><rb>一年</rb><rt>ih-nyin</rt><rb>雲</rb><rt>yüing</rt><rb>耽擱</rb><rt>tæn-koh</rt><rb>庉</rb><rt>deng</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>帳篷上</rb><rt>tsiang-bong-zông</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孫</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>庉</rb><rt>deng</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>營寨</rb><rt>ying-dza</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>拔營</rb><rt>bah-ying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>雲</rb><rt>yüing</rt><rb>上去個</rb><rt>zông-kyʽi-go</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>拔營</rb><rt>bah-ying</rt></ruby>。

<sup>23</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>奉</rb><rt>vong</rt><rb>耶和華個</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô-go</rt><rb>命令</rb><rt>ming-ling</rt><rb>搭營</rb><rt>tah-ying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>奉</rb><rt>vong</rt><rb>耶和華個</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô-go</rt><rb>命令</rb><rt>ming-ling</rt><rb>拔營</rb><rt>bah-ying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>保守</rb><rt>pao-siu</rt><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>Yiæ-ʽo-wô</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>摩西</u>個</rb><rt>Mo-si-go</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>。

### Ms. X.

<ruby><rb>耶和華</rb><rt>YIÆ-ʽO-WÔ</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：

<sup>2</sup> 「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>兩個</rb><rt>liang-go</rt><rb>號筒</rb><rt>ʽao-dong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>打鍊個</rb><rt>tang-lin-go</rt><rb>銀子</rb><rt>nying-ts</rt><rb>鑄造個</rb><rt>cü-zao-go</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>聚攏</rb><rt>jü-long</rt><rb>公會</rb><rt>kong-we</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>拔</rb><rt>bah</rt><rb>營寨個</rb><rt>ying-dza-go</rt><rb>用場</rb><rt>yüong-dziang</rt></ruby>。

<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>吹</rb><rt>Cʽü</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>號筒</rb><rt>ʽao-dong</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>全</rb><rt>djün</rt><rb>公會</rb><rt>kong-we</rt><rb>該</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>聚攏</rb><rt>jü-long</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>會幕</rb><rt>we-mo</rt><rb>門口</rb><rt>meng-kʽeo</rt></ruby>。

<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>Ziah</rt><rb>單</rb><rt>tæn</rt><rb>吹</rb><rt>cʽü</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>號筒</rb><rt>ʽao-dong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>keh-tsao</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>牧伯</rb><rt>moh-pah</rt><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>統帶</rb><rt>tʽong-ta</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>軍營個</rb><rt>kyüing-ying-go</rt><rb>該</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>走攏</rb><rt>tseo-long</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>。

<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>Ziah</rt><rb>吹</rb><rt>cʽü</rt><rb>帶</rb><rt>ta</rt><rb>長聲個</rb><rt>dziang-sing-go</rt><rb>號筒</rb><rt>ʽao-dong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>東邊個</rb><rt>tong-pin-go</rt><rb>營寨</rb><rt>ying-dza</rt><rb>該</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>先</rb><rt>sin</rt><rb>動身</rb><rt>dong-sing</rt></ruby>。

<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>第二遍</rb><rt>Di-nyi-pin</rt><rb>吹</rb><rt>cʽü</rt><rb>帶</rb><rt>ta</rt><rb>長聲個</rb><rt>dziang-sing-go</rt><rb>號筒</rb><rt>ʽao-dong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>南邊</rb><rt>nen-pin</rt><rb>營寨</rb><rt>ying-dza</rt><rb>該</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>動身</rb><rt>dong-sing</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>拔</rb><rt>bah</rt><rb>營個</rb><rt>ying-go</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>該</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>吹</rb><rt>cʽü</rt><rb>帶</rb><rt>ta</rt><rb>長聲</rb><rt>dziang-sing</rt><rb>號筒</rb><rt>ʽao-dong</rt></ruby>。

<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>召攏</rb><rt>Dziao-long</rt><rb>公會</rb><rt>kong-we</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>該</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>吹</rb><rt>cʽü</rt><rb>號筒</rb><rt>ʽao-dong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗要</rb><rt>feh-iao</rt><rb>帶</rb><rt>ta</rt><rb>長聲</rb><rt>dziang-sing</rt></ruby>。
